## List of undead

A list of the many known varieties of undead.

 Name Corporeality Sentience Short description Image Alhoon  Corporeal  Sentient  A form of mind flayer lich.   All-consuming hunger  Corporeal  Nonsentient  Actually a swarm composed of undead rotting body parts and organs. Always in motion and pulsating like a living heart.   Allip  Incorporeal  Nonsentient   Incorporeal remnants of people driven to insanity and suicide, spectral variations of the persons they once were.    Amiq rasol  Corporeal  Sentient  Undead that arose after being lost at sea, marooned, or murdered on the water and had, in life, rejected the gods.   Angel of decay  Corporeal  Sentient  Monsters powered by decay. Mockeries of true angels more by happenstance rather than design.    Archlich  Corporeal  Sentient  A special type of good-aligned lich.    Atropal  Corporeal  Sentient  Undead abominations said to be stillborn deities.    Baelnorn  Corporeal  Sentient  Undead elven spellcasters—similar to liches—who protected ancient elven sites.   Banedead  Corporeal  Sentient  Undead humanoids associated with the church of Bane recognizable by one hand being oversized and clawed.   Baneguard  Corporeal  Sentient  Skeletal spellcasters created by evil clerics.   Banelich  Corporeal  Sentient  A form of lich which served clerics of Bane.   Banshee  Incorporeal  Sentient  Female phantoms, typically from selfish, strong-willed spirit that embodied the essence of hideousness.   Barrowe  Corporeal  Sentient  Undead hill giants that bore great antipathy towards their living kin.   Bastellus  Incorporeal  Sentient  Creatures that fed on dream energies from sleeping folk.   Bhut  Incorporeal  Sentient  Poisonous undead that formed when a humanoid died alone, in a remote location, by violence. They hated the living and preyed on travellers, could possess corpses, and had an aversion to earth and stone  Blaspheme  Corporeal  Sentient    Bleakborn  Corporeal  Sentient    Blood amniote  Corporeal  Nonsentient  A mass of partly-congealed blood from many victims.   Blood fiend  Corporeal  Sentient  Horrors that fed on the blood of fiends the way vampires fed on humans.   Blood warrior  Corporeal  Sentient  Powerful undead warriors transformed through the dark magic of Kazgaroth.   Bloodkiss beholder  Corporeal  Sentient  A form of vampiric beholder.   Bloodmote cloud  Corporeal  Nonsentient    Bodak  Corporeal  Nonsentient  The undead remains of a humanoid who was destroyed by the touch of pure evil.   Bone naga  Corporeal  Sentient  Skeletal nagas, created by dark nagas, as guardians.   Bone rat swarm  Corporeal  Nonsentient    Bonebat  Corporeal  Sentient  Skeletal deep bats.   Boneless  Corporeal  Nonsentient  Undead unique to the Domains of Dread that arose from one or more corpses lacking a skeleton.   Boneyard  Corporeal  Sentient    Brain in a jar  Corporeal  Sentient  A disembodied, alchemically-preserved brain possessed of frightening intelligence and psionic powers.   Burnbones  Corporeal  Sentient  Creatures whos mortal frames had been dissolved and transformed from being infused with a portion of Cyrics divine power.   Caller in darkness  Incorporeal  Sentient  A psionic undead formed out of the minds of many who died at the same time.   Carcass eater  Corporeal  Nonsentient  Bestial undead that often dug up graves to eat corpses.   Chu-u  Incorporeal  Sentient  Legless ghost-like spirits found in Kara-tur.   Cinderspawn  Corporeal  Sentient    Coffer corpse  Corporeal  Sentient  A rare type of undead that arose when a humanoid was not interred as they had requested.   Coldlight walker  Corporeal  Nonsentient  The reanimated remains of a humanoid who died of cold and exposure.   Con-tinh  Incorporeal  Sentient  Undead spirits found in Kara-tur that formed from women who died too young.   Corpse rat swarm  Corporeal  Nonsentient    Crawling apocalypse  Corporeal  Nonsentient  A rare, mummy-like undead made from giant cephalopods that died when their home seas became deserts.   Crawling claw  Corporeal  Nonsentient  Reanimated severed hands.   Crawling head  Corporeal  Sentient  Reanimated head of a giant that crawls around on its intestines and arteries.  Crypt cat  Corporeal  Nonsentient  Mummified cats created to guard burial chambers.   Crypt chanter  Incorporeal  Sentient    Crypt servant  Corporeal  Sentient  Desiccated corpses that protected tombs in Zakhara.   Crypt thing  Corporeal  Sentient  An undead guardian of a particular location   Cryptknight  Corporeal  Sentient  The terrifying undead guardians of the Tomb of Martek.   Curst  Corporeal  Sentient  A form of undead created by a powerful curse.   Darklight  Incorporeal  Sentient  Undead creature created from the essence of the Quasi-Elemental Plane of Radiance.   Death knight  Corporeal  Sentient  Mighty undead warriors created by gods of death or other malevolent forces.   Death tyrant  Corporeal  Sentient  the result of a beholder's mad dreams of existing beyond death.   Deathfang  Corporeal  Nonsentient  Undead created from the body of a flying snake, sometimes taken on as familiars.   Deathlock  Corporeal  Sentient  Undead spellcasters bound to serve a master, usually the remains of warlocks reanimated by their patrons after failing to fulfill their part of a pact.   Demilich  Corporeal  Sentient  A lich who had evolved beyond a need for its undead body.   Desiccator  Corporeal  Sentient    Devourer  Corporeal  Sentient  Fiends who acted in the service of Orcus, to create armies of undead by consuming the bodies and souls of humanoids.   Direguard  Corporeal  Sentient  Skeletal spellcasters wreathed in shadowy armor. Closely related to baneguards.   Direhelm  Corporeal  Sentient  Creatures created using the foul rituals found in the Codex of Araunt. The only purpose of their existence was to serve their creators goals.   Doomsept  Corporeal  Sentient  wraith-like undead creatures created by fusing seven different spirits into one being.   Doomsphere  Incorporeal  Sentient  The ghost of a beholder.   Dracolich  Corporeal  Sentient  A dragon who had voluntarily tied its soul to a phylactery, freeing itself from death.   Draconic shard  Incorporeal  Sentient  Psionic remnant of a powerful gem dragon whose mind had persisted after death.   Dragonscale slough  Corporeal  Nonsentient  Slithering masses of dragon flesh and molted scales, infused with negative energy.  Dragonwight  Corporeal  Sentient  Dragon equivalents to wights, often in an advanced state of decomposition due to the time it took to prepare them.   Dread  Corporeal  Nonsentient  creatures in the form of a pair of skeletal arms from small- to medium-sized humanoids.   Dread warrior  Corporeal  Sentient  A powerful undead warrior created to serve in the armies of Szass Tam.   Dread wolf  Corporeal  Nonsentient  These creatures were often used as scouts, as they were conduits to their masters who could see and hear all that the dread wolves could.  Dream vestige  Incorporeal  Sentient  A mass of congealed souls that ever hunts for more souls to incorporate into itself.   Eidolon  Corporeal  Nonsentient  Undead constructs that were designed for the sole purpose of protecting sites considered sacred or important to deities or archdevils.   Ephemeral swarm  Incorporeal  Nonsentient  Swarms of small, ghostly creatures that arose when a large number of small creatures died a violent death at the same time.   Entomber  Corporeal  Sentient?    Entropic reaper  Corporeal  Sentient    Eye of fear and flame  Corporeal  Sentient  Created by chaotic evil deities for the purpose of spreading darkness and destruction, as well as fomenting the downfall of lawful creatures.   Flameskull  Corporeal  Sentient  Fiery undead guardians fashioned from the skulls of dead spellcasters.   Forgewraith  Incorporeal  Sentient  Undead that arose when one or multiple evil humanoids died in fire.   Forsaken shell  Corporeal  Sentient  Undead consisting of a full set of skin that had been removed from the body of a slain or still-living creature.   Gaki, jiki-ketsu-gaki  Incorporeal  Sentient  Reincarnated spirits of wicked people, specifically  priests who committed heresy. They were reincarnated as gaki for punishment for living a life of evil and sin.   Gaki, jiki-niku-gaki  Incorporeal  Sentient  Evil spirits looking like emaciated humanoids, with dry peeling skin. They were ambush predators, whos overwhelming urge to feed often drove them to attack openly or recklessly.  Gaki, shikki-gaki  Incorporeal  Sentient  Evil spirits resembling emaciated, disease-ridden humanoids, with grey rotting skin and open sores. They were vicious fighters using their sharp claws to kill.  Gaki, shinen-gaki  Incorporeal  Sentient  Spirits in the form of small floating balls of either blue, red, white, or yellow flame. They were reincarnated spirits of wicked people, of soldiers who committed treason or acts of cowardice.  Gaunt  Corporeal  Nonsentient  A variety of undead horse, they were most often companions to night riders and were prominently used by them during the Time of Troubles. Thought to be raised only by the gods Bane and Myrkul.   Ghost  Incorporeal  Sentient  The souls of deceased sentient creatures that had been bound to haunt a specific area, object, or creature that held significance to them when they were alive.   Ghost dragon  Incorporeal  Sentient  The ghost of a dragon whose hoard was looted.   Ghost mount  Incorporeal  Sentient  Spirits of two forms: true and illusory. Their true forms were a transparent outline of what they were in life such as horses, camels, or antelopes. To hide their real form they used powerful illusory spells.   Ghoul  Corporeal  Sentient  Undead humanoids that fed on the flesh of humanoids.   Ghoul, abyssal  Corporeal  Sentient  Twisted and slightly fiendish ghouls that served Kiaransalee.   Ghoul, flesh seeker  Corporeal  Sentient  A type of ghoul that arose from the bodies of cannabilistic cultists of Orcus.   Ghoul, ghast  Corporeal  Sentient  More powerful than regular ghouls, ghasts were distinguished by their foul stench.   Ghoul, gravetouched  Corporeal  Sentient  Ghouls that retained the abilities they had in life, and were therefore said to have been blessed by Orcus.  Ghoul, lacedon  Corporeal  Sentient  Aquatic ghouls that swam as easily as they walked.   Ghul-kin, soultaker  Corporeal  Sentient  Shapechangers that could change their physical form at will, usually to that of an attractive humanoid. Immune to weapons made of any material save wood, and able to possess victims if they were able to kiss them on the lips.  Ghul-kin, witherer  Corporeal  Sentient  Witherer's touch weakened targets while strengthening themselves. In addition to draining strength, each touch also caused harm and aged the victim by one year.   Grave mist  Incorporeal  Sentient   Gravehound  Corporeal  Nonsentient    Great ghul  Corporeal  Sentient  Undeaed cousins of genies who occupied the lowest rung in the hierarchy of the jann, they adored eating the flesh from corpses and then drinking the bone marrow.   Haures  Corporeal  Sentient  Quasi-undead demons created by Orcus from the corpses of goristros that had died defending him. They acted as leaders, frontline soldiers, and executioners for their demon-lord.   Heucuva  Corporeal  Sentient  Damned undead that formed from druids, priests, monks, or paladins that forsook their vows.   Hoarder dragon  Incorporeal  Sentient  Undead formed from dragons so greedy, they would not leave their hoards even in death.   Hullathoin  Corporeal  Sentient  Massive, animal-like undead covered with a wasting disease; typically commands swarms of lesser undead.  Hulking corpse  Corporeal  Nonsentient    Immolith  Corporeal  Sentient  Undead demons that randomly arose from the spirits of deceased demons. After having been slain and returned to their foul plane, the once-separate demonic spirits fused together in the fall back to the Abyss, given new form and burning hatred.   Inquisitor  Corporeal  Sentient  Undead that excelled and delighted in torturing living beings.   Keening spirit  Incorporeal  ?  Ghost-like undead that were created from evil elven females. They were also called kiaranshee.   Kuei  Incorporeal  Sentient  Spirits found in Kara-tur that formed when a person died a violent end, with something left undone.   Lich  Corporeal  Sentient  Nefarious form of undead spellcasters of great power, usually wizards, but also sorcerers, warlocks, and clerics. Liches were feared by mortal beings for their mastery of malign magic, their intelligence, and their willingness to embrace undeath for a chance to live forever—or rather, to endure forever.   Lich, elemental  Corporeal  Sentient  A form of lichdom achievable only by elementalists.   Mummy  Corporeal  Sentient  Creatures wrapped in old strips of linen damaged by rot and age. Any creature they touched was affected by a rotting curse called mummy rot.   Salt mummy  Corporeal  Sentient  Creatures that arose from the bodies of those who were buried too close to veins of salt, and died carrying a great sin, the horror of which gradually created a linkage to the Negative Energy plane.   Mohrg  Corporeal  Sentient  The risen corpses of mass murderers, mohrgs looked like skeletons containing only their viscera.   Murk  Incorporeal  Sentient?  A free-roaming blot of undeath that sought to drain life energy.  Necromental  Corporeal  Nonsentient  An undead remnant of an elemental.   Necropolitan  Corporeal  Sentient  Humanoids who embraced undeath by way of a particular ritual called the Ritual of Crucimigration.  Night rider  Corporeal  Sentient  More powerful and dangerous than ordinary zombies or skeletons, they were used as guardians or servants by more powerful undead or even by the god Myrkul.   Nightshade, nightwalker  Corporeal  Sentient  Undead monsters from the Plane of Shadow, also known as the Shadowfell, they took delight in the corruption and suffering of others. Creatures of both death and shadows who had enormous powers over darkness.   Nightshade, nightcrawler  Corporeal  Sentient  Resembling purple worms, but covered in solid black, chitinous armor. Even their teeth and the insides of the mouth were black.   Nightshade, nighthaunt  Corporeal  Sentient    Nightshade, nightwing  Corporeal  Sentient    Penanggalan  Corporeal  Sentient  A variety of female only vampiric undead creatures. In the day, they appeared as human females resembling their original form before undeath. At night, they  returned to their lairs and their heads separated from their bodies, revealing a  long tail.   Plague blight  Corporeal  Sentient  The animated corpses of humanoids who died of plague or rot.  Poltergeist  Incorporeal  Sentient  Invisible to all except those who had the ability to see the invisible, their sole purpose was to inflict suffering on the living. This undying, destructive rage was fueled by their confusion over having no sense of how they had died.   Quell  Incorporeal  Sentient  Undead that hated all deities and their servants, and whose very presence interfered with divine magic.   Quth-maren  Corporeal  Sentient  Flayed undead corpses that oozed acid and served Kiaransalee and her worshipers.   Raiment  Corporeal  Nonsentient  The clothing of a victim killed in some horrific manner, animated by the vengeful spirit.  Rathrea  Incorporeal  Sentient?  Sometimes called "ghost dragons", rathrea formed when a dragon ate too much arambar.  Revenant  Corporeal  Sentient  Undead who tirelessly hunted those against whom they had sworn revenge.   Reviler  Incorporeal  ?    Revived fossil  Corporeal  Nonsentient    Rom  Corporeal  Sentient  Undead giants found in Zakhara.   Seawraith  Corporeal?  ?    Seven Wanderers  Corporeal  Sentient   Shadow  Incorporeal  ? Skin kite  Corporeal  Nonsentient    Skirr  Corporeal  Nonsentient  Beast-like undead predators that hunted near necropolises.   Skulking cyst  Corporeal  Sentient    Skull lord  Corporeal  Sentient    Skuz  Corporeal  ?   Slaymate  Corporeal  Sentient  Undead that arose when a child died because of their guardian's betrayal or negligence.  Slow shadow  Incorporeal  Nonsentient   Spawn of Kyuss  Corporeal  Sentient   Specter  Incorporeal  Sentient   Spectral harpist  Incorporeal  Sentient  The spirit of a dead Master Harper.  Spectral lyrist  Incorporeal  Sentient  Undead who, in life, used their songs to sow strife and violence, and for such crimes cursed to walk as undead.  Spectral mage  Incorporeal  Sentient   Spectral troll  Incorporeal  Sentient   Tomb dwarf  ?  ?   Tomb mote  Corporeal  Sentient?   Topi  Corporeal  ?    Ulgurstasta  Corporeal  Sentient  A massive, maggot-like undead first invented by Kyuss. Ur-histachii  Corporeal  Nonsentient   Vampire  Corporeal  Sentient  Infamous bloodsuckers.  Vampire, corpse  Corporeal  Sentient  Also called pouncing vampires, corpse vampires arose when a humanoid was buried improperly. Like other vampires, they fed on blood. ## List of dishes (in-universe)

This is a list of dishes known to exist in the Realms, excluding breads, drinks, sauces, and desserts. It includes information on the cuisine of the dish, establishments and cities where they could be found, and each of the dish's main ingredients. Cuisines are broken down into the nation of origin, or general area of the content that has shared culinary influences, and well as racial cuisines for non-human people of the Realms.

{{Quote|Low Tier: "Another Rescue"
The Midsummer's Festival is attacked. A visiting High Priestess is kidnapped. Deputy Mayor O'Kane needs your help to recover this priestess before the Red Wizards get their hands on her. This adventure is designed for low- and mid-level characters.
High Tier: "Dealing With Darkness"
The Midsummer's Festival has come to Ravens Bluff again. You've been invited to enjoy yourself in the Temple District, while others of you have been asked to provide security during the event. But just what dark menace may rear its ugly head in this atmosphere of good cheer? Are you willing to find out? This adventure is designed for high-level characters.}}
